y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    \n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n    \n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = c[idx];\n                \n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * \n                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line         printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option:      // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(cold, cnew);\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\nproject(cahn_hilliard_benchmark)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const stencil kernels.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int ZCHUNK = 8;\n\n// 3D index calculation (host side, global field)\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Local slabs are stored with one ghost plane on each side in Z:\n// local plane z (0 <= z < nzloc) lives at device offset (z + 1) * nx * ny.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const long long z, const long long nx, const long long ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(nx * ny);\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx, const int ny, const int z0, const int z1,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n    const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    // Clamped (zero-gradient) boundaries in X and Y\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx;\n    const size_t self = row + x;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = c + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double cm = p[self - plane];\n    double cv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = c + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double cp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double cxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n        const double cyy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;\n        const double czz = (cp + cm - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) //\n                                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv                                      //\n                                            - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cm = cv;\n        cv = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx,\n    const int ny, const int z0, const int z1, const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy,\n    const double dz) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx;\n    const size_t self = row + x;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = mu + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double mm = p[self - plane];\n    double mv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = mu + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double mp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double mxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * mv) / dx2;\n        const double myy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * mv) / dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mp + mm - 2.0 * mv) / dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mm = mv;\n        mv = mp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (values identical to the serial reference)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long z = 0; z < static_cast<long long>(nzloc); ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(z), nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (static_cast<size_t>(z) + zoff) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const long long n = static_cast<long long>(c.size());\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nnamespace {\n\n// Everything needed to advance one local slab on one GPU.\nstruct Slab {\n    int nx = 0, ny = 0, nzloc = 0;\n    size_t plane = 0;     // nx * ny\n    size_t bytesPlane = 0;\n\n    double* d_cold = nullptr;\n    double* d_cnew = nullptr;\n    double* d_mu = nullptr;\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers (used when the MPI stack is not CUDA aware)\n    double* h_sendLo = nullptr;\n    double* h_sendHi = nullptr;\n    double* h_recvLo = nullptr;\n    double* h_recvHi = nullptr;\n\n    cudaStream_t sCompute = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t sHalo = nullptr;\n\n    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    int lo = MPI_PROC_NULL; // neighbour towards smaller z\n    int hi = MPI_PROC_NULL; // neighbour towards larger z\n    bool cudaAware = false;\n\n    double* plane_ptr(double* base, const int z) const { return base + static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * plane; }\n};\n\nvoid launchMu(const Slab& s, const double* c, double* mu, const int z0, const int z1, const double dx, const double dy,\n              const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n              cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (z1 <= z0) return;\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid((s.nx + BX - 1) / BX, (s.ny + BY - 1) / BY, (z1 - z0 + ZCHUNK - 1) / ZCHUNK);\n    computeChemicalPotentialKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(c, mu, s.nx, s.ny, z0, z1, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB,\n                                                               e_AB);\n}\n\nvoid launchUpdate(const Slab& s, double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu, const int z0, const int z1,\n                  const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                  cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (z1 <= z0) return;\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid((s.nx + BX - 1) / BX, (s.ny + BY - 1) / BY, (z1 - z0 + ZCHUNK - 1) / ZCHUNK);\n    cahnHilliardUpdateKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cnew, cold, mu, s.nx, s.ny, z0, z1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n}\n\n// Fill the ghost planes of `field`: MPI halo exchange with the z-neighbours and, at the global\n// domain boundaries, a mirror of the outermost plane (which reproduces the clamped stencil).\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field) {\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc), s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1), s.bytesPlane,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, s.sHalo));\n    }\n\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL && s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double* sendLo = s.plane_ptr(field, 0);              // to lower neighbour\n    const double* sendHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1);    // to upper neighbour\n    double* recvLo = s.plane_ptr(field, -1);                   // from lower neighbour\n    double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = recvHi;\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendLo, sendLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendHi, sendHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        sbufLo = s.h_sendLo;\n        sbufHi = s.h_sendHi;\n        rbufLo = s.h_recvLo;\n        rbufHi = s.h_recvHi;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(s.plane);\n    MPI_Request reqs[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufLo, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufLo), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufHi, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufHi), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvLo, s.h_recvLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvHi, s.h_recvHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // --- 1D domain decomposition along Z -------------------------------------------------\n    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = nz % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t nzloc = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t zoff = base * static_cast<size_t>(rank) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n\n    // Ranks without any planes (more ranks than z-planes) stay out of the halo exchange but\n    // still take part in the global collectives.\n    const int active = (nzloc > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    MPI_Comm activeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, active, rank, &activeComm);\n\n    // --- Pick a GPU: one per rank, round-robin within the node ---------------------------\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int numDevices = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&numDevices));\n    if (numDevices == 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % numDevices));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); // establish the context before timing\n\n    Slab slab;\n    slab.nx = static_cast<int>(nx);\n    slab.ny = static_cast<int>(ny);\n    slab.nzloc = static_cast<int>(nzloc);\n    slab.plane = nx * ny;\n    slab.bytesPlane = slab.plane * sizeof(double);\n    slab.comm = activeComm;\n#if defined(MPIX_CUDA_AWARE_SUPPORT) && MPIX_CUDA_AWARE_SUPPORT\n    slab.cudaAware = (MPIX_Query_cuda_support() == 1);\n#else\n    slab.cudaAware = false;\n#endif\n\n    std::vector<double> local(slab.plane * nzloc);\n    if (active) {\n        int arank = 0, asize = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(activeComm, &arank);\n        MPI_Comm_size(activeComm, &asize);\n        slab.lo = (arank > 0) ? arank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        slab.hi = (arank + 1 < asize) ? arank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n        const size_t bytes = slab.plane * (nzloc + 2) * sizeof(double);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&slab.d_cold, bytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&slab.d_cnew, bytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&slab.d_mu, bytes));\n        if (!slab.cudaAware) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_sendLo, slab.bytesPlane));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_sendHi, slab.bytesPlane));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_recvLo, slab.bytesPlane));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_recvHi, slab.bytesPlane));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&slab.sCompute));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&slab.sHalo));\n\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n        initializeConcentration(local, nx, ny, nz, zoff, nzloc);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(slab.plane_ptr(slab.d_cold, 0), local.data(), local.size() * sizeof(double),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    } else if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    if (active) {\n        const int nzl = slab.nzloc;\n        // Interior planes do not touch the ghost layers and are computed while the halo\n        // exchange for the boundary planes is in flight.\n        const int i0 = (nzl >= 3) ? 1 : 0;\n        const int i1 = (nzl >= 3) ? nzl - 1 : 0;\n\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Chemical potential\n            launchMu(slab, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, i0, i1, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, slab.sCompute);\n            exchangeHalos(slab, slab.d_cold);\n            if (nzl >= 3) {\n                launchMu(slab, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, 0, 1, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, slab.sCompute);\n                launchMu(slab, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, nzl - 1, nzl, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB,\n                         slab.sCompute);\n            } else {\n                launchMu(slab, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, 0, nzl, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, slab.sCompute);\n            }\n\n            // Concentration update\n            launchUpdate(slab, slab.d_cnew, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, i0, i1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz, slab.sCompute);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(slab.sCompute)); // mu boundary planes must be ready\n            exchangeHalos(slab, slab.d_mu);\n            if (nzl >= 3) {\n                launchUpdate(slab, slab.d_cnew, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, 0, 1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz, slab.sCompute);\n                launchUpdate(slab, slab.d_cnew, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, nzl - 1, nzl, D, dt, dx, dy, dz, slab.sCompute);\n            } else {\n                launchUpdate(slab, slab.d_cnew, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, 0, nzl, D, dt, dx, dy, dz, slab.sCompute);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n            std::swap(slab.d_cold, slab.d_cnew);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(slab.sCompute));\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    const auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance\n        const double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n        const double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    }\n\n    // --- Gather the full field on rank 0 when results are needed --------------------------\n    std::vector<double> full;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        if (active) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(local.data(), slab.plane_ptr(slab.d_cold, 0), local.size() * sizeof(double),\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        }\n        if (rank == 0) full.resize(gridSize);\n\n        std::vector<int> counts(nranks), displs(nranks);\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n            const size_t n = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            counts[r] = static_cast<int>(n * slab.plane);\n            displs[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += n * slab.plane;\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? full.data() : nullptr, counts.data(),\n                    displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    int status = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(full, \"Concentration\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            const bool valid = validateResult(full, nx, ny, nz);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (active) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(slab.d_cold));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(slab.d_cnew));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(slab.d_mu));\n        if (!slab.cudaAware) \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do { \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", stencil kernels.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int ZCHUNK = 8;\n\n// 3D index calculation (host side, global field)\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Local slabs are stored with one ghost plane on each side in Z:\n// local plane z (0 <= z < nzloc) lives at device offset (z + 1) * nx * ny.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const long long z, const long long nx, const long long ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(nx * ny);\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx, const int ny, const int z0, const int z1,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n    const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    // Clamped (zero-gradient) boundaries in X and Y\n    const * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = c + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double cm = p[self - plane];\n    double cv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = c + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double cp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double czz = (cp + cm - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) //\n                                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv                                      //\n                                            - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cm = cv;\n        cv = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx,\n    const int ny, const int z0, const int z1, const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy,\n    const double dz) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = mu + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double mm = p[self - plane];\n    double mv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = mu + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double mp = q[self + dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mp + mm - 2.0 * mv) / dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mm = mv;\n        mv = mp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (values identical to the serial reference)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long z = 0; z < static_cast<long long>(nzloc); ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y,          const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const long long n = static_cast<long long>(c.size());\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\",               const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                  cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (z1 <= z0) return;\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n}\n\n// Fill the ghost planes of `field`: MPI halo exchange with the z-neighbours and, at the global\n// domain boundaries, a mirror of the outermost plane (which reproduces the clamped stencil).\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field) {\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendHi, sendHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        sbufLo = s.h_sendLo;\n        sbufHi = s.h_sendHi;\n        rbufLo = s.h_recvLo;\n        rbufHi = s.h_recvHi;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(s.plane);\n    MPI_Request reqs[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufLo, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufLo), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufHi, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufHi), count, MPI_DOUBLE, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvLo, s.h_recvLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvHi, s.h_recvHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t nx                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    } else if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Local slabs are stored with one ghost plane on each side in Z:\n// local plane z (0 <= z < nzloc) lives at device offset (z + 1) * nx * ny.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const long long z, const long long nx, const long long ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(nx * ny);\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx, const int ny, const int z0, const int z1,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n    const >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = c + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double cp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double cxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n        const double cyy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;\n        const double czz = (cp + cm - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(z,                                         - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cm = cv;\n        cv = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx,\n    const int ny, const int z0, static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx;\n    const size_t self = row + x;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = mu + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double mm = p[self - plane];\n    double mv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = mu + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double mp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double mxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * mv) / dx2;\n        const double myy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * mv) / dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mp + mm - 2.0 * mv) / dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mm = mv;\n        mv = mp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (values identical to the serial reference)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long z = 0; z < static_cast<long long>(nzloc); ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(z), nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (static_cast<size_t>(z) + zoff) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const long long n = static_cast<long long>(c.size());\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nnamespace {\n\n// Everything needed to advance one local slab on one GPU.\nstruct Slab {\n    int nx = 0, ny = 0, nzloc = 0;\n    size_t plane = 0;     // nx * ny\n    size_t bytesPlane = 0;\n\n    double* d_cold = nullptr;\n    double* d_cnew = nullptr;\n    double* d_mu = nullptr;\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers (used when the MPI stack is not CUDA aware)\n    double* h_sendLo = nullptr;\n    double* h_sendHi = nullptr;\n    double* h_recvLo = nullptr;\n    double* h_recvHi = nullptr;\n\n    cudaStream_t sCompute = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t sHalo = nullptr;\n\n    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    int lo = MPI_PROC_NULL; // neighbour towards smaller z\n    int hi = MPI_PROC_NULL; // neighbour towards larger z\n    bool cudaAware = false;\n\n    double* plane_ptr(double* base, const int z) const { return base + static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * plane; }\n};\n\nvoid launchMu(const Slab& s, const double* c, double* mu, const int z0, const int z1, const double dx, const double dy,\n              const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,\n              cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (z1 <= z0) return;\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid((s.nx + BX - 1) / BX, (s.ny + BY - 1) / BY, (z1 - z0 + ZCHUNK - 1) / ZCHUNK);\n    computeChemicalPotentialKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(c, mu, s.nx, s.ny, z0, z1, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB,\n                                                               e_AB);\n}\n\nvoid launchUpdate(const Slab& s, double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu, const int z0, const int z1,\n                  const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                  cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (z1 <= z0) return;\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid((s.nx + BX - 1) / BX, (s.ny + BY - 1) / BY, (z1 - z0 + ZCHUNK - 1) / ZCHUNK);\n    cahnHilliardUpdateKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cnew, cold, mu, s.nx, s.ny, z0, z1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n}\n\n// Fill the ghost planes of `field`: MPI halo exchange with the z-neighbours and, at the global\n// domain boundaries, a mirror of the outermost plane (which reproduces the clamped stencil).\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field) {\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc), s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1), s.bytesPlane,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, s.sHalo));\n    }\n\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL && s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double* sendLo = s.plane_ptr(field, 0);              // to lower neighbour\n    const double* sendHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1);    // to upper neighbour\n    double* recvLo = s.plane_ptr(field, -1);                   // from lower neighbour\n    double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = recvHi;\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendLo, sendLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendHi, sendHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        sbufLo = s.h_sendLo;\n        sbufHi = s.h_sendHi;\n        rbufLo = s.h_recvLo;\n        rbufHi = s.h_recvHi;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(s.plane);\n    MPI_Request reqs[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufLo, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufLo), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufHi, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufHi), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvLo, s.h_recvLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvHi, s.h_recvHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n static_cast<size_t>(rank) + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n\n    // Ranks without any planes (more ranks than z-planes) stay out of the halo exchange but\n    // still take part in the size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Local slabs are stored with one ghost plane on each side in Z:\n// local plane z (0 <= z < nzloc) lives at device offset (z + 1) * nx * ny.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const long long z, const long long nx, const long long ny) {\n    return void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx, const int ny, const int z0, const int z1,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n    const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    // Clamped (zero-gradient) boundaries in X and Y\n    const static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx;\n    const size_t self = row + x;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = c + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double cm = p[self - plane];\n    double cv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = c + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double cp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double cxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n        const double cyy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;\n        const + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv                                      //\n                                            - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cm = cv;\n        cv = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx,\n    const int ny, const int z0, const int z1, const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy,\n    const double dz) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx;\n    const size_t self = row + x;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = mu + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double mm = p[self - plane];\n    double mv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = mu + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double mp = q[self + dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mp + mm - 2.0 * mv) / dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mm = mv;\n        mv = mp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (values identical to the serial reference)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long z = 0; z < static_cast<long long>(nzloc); ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y,          const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const long long n = static_cast<long long>(c.size());\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n advance one local slab on one GPU.\nstruct Slab {\n    int nx = 0, ny = 0, nzloc = 0;\n    size_t plane = 0;     // nx * ny\n    size_t bytesPlane = 0;\n\n    double* d_cold = nullptr;\n    double* double* h_recvLo = nullptr;\n    double* h_recvHi = nullptr;\n\n    cudaStream_t sCompute = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t sHalo = nullptr;\n\n    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    int lo = base + static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * plane; }\n};\n\nvoid launchMu(const Slab& s, const double* c, double* mu, const int z0, const int z1, const double dx, const double dy,\n              const double e_BB,\n                                                               e_AB);\n}\n\nvoid launchUpdate(const Slab& s, double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu, const int z0, const int z1,\n                  const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                  cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (z1 <= z0) return;\n    const dim3 block(BX, BY, 1);\n    const dim3 grid((s.nx + BX - 1) / BX, (s.ny + BY - 1) / BY, (z1 - z0 + ZCHUNK - 1) / ZCHUNK);\n    cahnHilliardUpdateKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cnew, cold, mu, s.nx, s.ny, z0, z1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n}\n\n// Fill the ghost planes of `field`: MPI halo exchange with the z-neighbours and, at the global\n// domain boundaries, a mirror of the outermost plane (which reproduces the clamped stencil).\n// `ready` marks the point in the compute stream at which the outermost planes of `field` are\n// final; the halo stream waits for it, so it can run concurrently with interior compute.\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field, cudaEvent_t ready) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.sHalo, ready, 0));\n\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc), s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1), s.bytesPlane,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, s.sHalo));\n    }\n\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL && s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double* sendLo = s.plane_ptr(field, 0);              // to lower neighbour\n    const double* sendHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1);    // to upper neighbour\n    double* recvLo = s.plane_ptr(field, -1);                   // from lower neighbour\n    double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = recvHi;\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendLo, sendLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendHi, sendHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        sbufLo = s.h_sendLo;\n        sbufHi = s.h_sendHi;\n        rbufLo = s.h_recvLo;\n        rbufHi = s.h_recvHi;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(s.plane);\n    MPI_Request reqs[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufLo, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufLo), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufHi, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufHi), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvLo, s.h_recvLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvHi, s.h_recvHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // --- 1D domain decomposition along Z -------------------------------------------------\n    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = nz % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n any planes (more ranks than z-planes) stay out of the halo exchange but\n    // still take part in the global collectives.\n    const int active = (nzloc > 0) ? 1 : 0;\n    MPI_Comm activeComm =    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n std::vector<double> local(slab.plane * nzloc);\n    if (active) {\n        int arank = 0, asize = 0;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(activeComm, &arank);\n        MPI_Comm_size(activeComm, &asize);\n        slab.lo = (arank > 0) ? arank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n        slab.hi = (arank + 1 < asize) ? arank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n        const size_t bytes = slab.plane * (nzloc + 2) * sizeof(double);\n // exchange for the boundary planes is in flight.\n        const int i0 = (nzl >= 3) ? 1 : 0;\n        const int i1 = (nzl >= 3) ? nzl - 1 : 0;\n\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n e_AB, slab.sCompute);\n            }\n\n            // Concentration update\n            launchUpdate(slab, slab.d_cnew, slab.d_cold, slab.d_mu, i0, i1, D, dt, dx, dy, dz, slab.sCompute);\n displs[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += n * slab.plane;\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? full.data() : nullptr, counts.data(),\n    print_results(full, \"Concentration\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            const bool valid = Thread block / register blocking configuration for the marching stencil kernels.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int ZCHUNK = 8;\n\n// 3D index calculation (host side, global field)\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Local slabs are stored with one ghost plane on each side in Z:\n// local plane z (0 <= z < nzloc) lives at device offset (z + 1) * nx * ny.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const long long z, const long long nx, const long long ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z + 1) * static_cast<size_t>(nx * ny);\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx, const int ny, const int z0, const int z1,\n    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB,\n    const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    // Clamped (zero-gradient) boundaries in X and Y\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double cp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double cxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n        const double cyy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;\n        const double czz = (cp + cm - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) //\n                                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv                                      //\n                                            - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cm = cv;\n        cv = cp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold, const double* __restrict__ mu, const int nx,\n    const int ny, const int z0, const int z1, const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy,\n    const double dz) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int zstart = z0 + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * ZCHUNK;\n    if (zstart >= z1) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx;\n    const size_t self = row + x;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    const double* p = mu + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double mm = p[self - plane];\n    double mv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n        if (z >= z1) break;\n\n        const double* q = mu + planeOffset(z, nx, ny);\n        const double mp = q[self + plane];\n\n        const double mxx = (q[row + xp] + q[row + xn] - 2.0 * mv) / dx2;\n        const double myy = (q[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * nx + x] + q[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * nx + x] - 2.0 * mv) / dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mp + mm - 2.0 * mv) / dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(z, nx, ny) + self;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + dt * D * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mm = mv;\n        mv = mp;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize the local slab of the concentration field (values identical to the serial reference)\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                             const size_t zoff, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long z = 0; z < static_cast<long long>(nzloc); ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(z), nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = (static_cast<size_t>(z) + zoff) * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const long long n = static_cast<long long>(c.size());\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of are\n// final; the halo stream waits for it, so it can run concurrently with interior compute.\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field, cudaEvent_t ready) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.sHalo, ready, 0));\n\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc), s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1), s.bytesPlane,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, s.sHalo));\n    }\n\n to lower neighbour\n    const double* sendHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1);    // to upper neighbour\n    double* recvLo = s.plane_ptr(field, -1);                   // from lower neighbour\n    double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = recvHi;\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendLo, sendLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendHi, sendHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        sbufLo = s.h_sendLo;\n        sbufHi = s.h_sendHi;\n        rbufLo CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvLo, s.h_recvLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvHi, s.h_recvHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n Dividing by h^2 is exactly equivalent to multiplying by\n// 1/h^2 whenever h^2 is a power of two, which lets us avoid the (very slow) FP64 division on\n// the device for the usual uniform unit spacing. RECIP selects the exact-reciprocal variant.\ntemplate <bool RECIP>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleH2(const double v, const double h2) {\n    return RECIP ? v * h2 : v / h2;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\ntemplate <bool RECIP>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n    const = c + planeOffset(zstart, nx, ny);\n    double cm = p[self - plane];\n    double cv = p[self];\n\n#pragma unroll 1\n    for (int k = 0; k < ZCHUNK; ++k) {\n        const int z = zstart + k;\n                    const size_t zoff, const size_t nzloc) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long z = 0; z < static_cast<long long>(nzloc); ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, static_cast<size_t>(z), nx, ny);\n ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny,\n                    [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const long long n = static_cast<long long>(c.size());\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int bad = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(| : bad)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(c[i]) || std::isinf(c[i])) bad = 1;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f,         e_AB);\n}\n\nvoid launchUpdate(const Slab& s, double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu, const int z0, const int z1,\n                  const double D, const double dt, const double halo exchange with the z-neighbours and, at the global\n// domain boundaries, a mirror of the outermost plane (which reproduces the clamped stencil).\n// `ready` marks the point in the compute stream at which the outermost planes of `field` are\n// final; the halo stream waits for it, so it can run concurrently with interior compute.\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field, cudaEvent_t ready) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.sHalo, ready, 0));\n\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc), s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1), s.bytesPlane,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, s.sHalo));\n    }\n\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL && s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double* sendLo = s.plane_ptr(field, 0);              // to lower neighbour\n    const double* sendHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc - 1);    // to upper neighbour\n    double* recvLo = s.plane_ptr(field, -1);                   // from lower neighbour\n    double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = recvHi;\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendLo, sendLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.h_sendHi, sendHi, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.sHalo));\n        sbufLo = s.h_sendLo;\n        sbufHi = s.h_sendHi;\n        rbufLo = s.h_recvLo;\n        rbufHi = s.h_recvHi;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.sHalo));\n\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(s.plane);\n    MPI_Request reqs[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufLo, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufLo), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.lo, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(rbufHi, count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 0, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(const_cast<void*>(sbufHi), count, MPI_DOUBLE, s.hi, 1, s.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!s.cudaAware) {\n        if (s.lo != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvLo, s.h_recvLo, s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.sHalo));\n        if (s.hi != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(recvHi, s.h_recvHi, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_sendHi, slab.bytesPlane));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_recvLo, slab.bytesPlane));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&slab.h_recvHi,                     cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    } else if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard Dividing by h^2 is exactly equivalent to multiplying by\n// 1/h^2 whenever h^2 is a power of two, which lets us avoid the (very slow) FP64 division on\n// the device for the usual uniform unit spacing. RECIP selects the exact-reciprocal variant.\ntemplate <bool RECIP>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleH2(const double v, const double h2) {\n    return RECIP ? v * h2 : v / h2;\n}\n\n// Chemical z-neighbours and, at the global\n// domain boundaries, a mirror of the outermost plane (which reproduces the clamped stencil).\n// `ready` marks the point in the compute stream at which the outermost planes of `field` are\n// final; the halo stream waits for it, so it can run concurrently with interior compute.\nvoid exchangeHalos(Slab& s, double* field, cudaEvent_t ready) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.sHalo, ready, 0));\n\n    // Mirror at global boundaries (no neighbour in that direction)\n    if (s.lo == MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(s.plane_ptr(field, -1), s.plane_ptr(field, 0), s.bytesPlane, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice,\n                                   s.sHalo));\n    }\n    if (s.hi == MPI_PROC_NULL) double* recvHi = s.plane_ptr(field, s.nzloc);              // from upper neighbour\n\n    const void* sbufLo = sendLo;\n    const void* sbufHi = sendHi;\n    void* rbufLo = recvLo;\n    void* rbufHi = Dividing by h^2 is exactly equivalent to multiplying by\n// 1/h^2 whenever h^2 is a power of two, which lets us avoid the (very slow) FP64 division on\n// the device for the usual uniform unit spacing. RECIP selects the exact-reciprocal variant.\ntemplate <bool RECIP>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double scaleH2(const double v, const double h2) {\n    return RECIP ? v * h2 : v / h2;\n}\n\n// Chemical
